The design contract (design-spec §11 / OQ-2\n    # D-0082 §4 row E15) requires the harness to:\n    #   (a) reap the slow hook at the configured timeout boundary — the\n    #       tool call MUST complete within `hook_timeout + 2.0` seconds\n    #       (the +2.0s grace covers reap-overhead, not steady-state slop);\n    #   (b) complete the matched tool call successfully despite the\n    #       hook timeout — the tool's result reaches the agent (fail-\n    #       open disposition);\n    #   (c) emit a structured `{type:\"hook_timeout\", disposition:\n    #       \"fail_open\"}` row to `logs/hook-errors.jsonl` so the timeout\n    #       event is auditable post-run.\n    # E15 sits in `hook-lifecycle` category alongside E3-E13 — it pins\n    # the timeout-handling branch of the error-half of the lifecycle,\n    # parallel to E13's stderr-error branch.
